# Image Prompts: 25 One-Paycheck Jobs That Could Buy a House in 1960s America (Now Gone Forever)

Script: `script.md` (locked, word for word). 544 beats, about 31 min at 2.75 words/s, each beat 3-5 s or shorter.

## Part A: Mix check
```
Beats: 544 | 🎞️ film 70 | 📷 photo 169 | 📰 ad 5 | 🎬 stock 63 | 🔢 graphic 104 | 🎨 AI 133
Real share: 56% ✅ (target ≥55%) | AI share: 24% ✅ (target ≤25%)
```

**How to read each beat.** Every beat has a standalone image prompt. On 🎞️ 📷 📰 🎬 beats, download the real footage first and use the prompt only if the search finds nothing. On 🎨 and 🔢 beats, the prompt is the visual. Brand names, dates and numbers go on as CapCut text, never inside an AI image. If any AI image looks like a real historical photo, tick YouTube's **"altered or synthetic content" = Yes**.

**Style modes** (from the Visual Style Profile): A = B&W era photo · B = archival film frame · C = faded 70s-80s colour · D = grid-paper cut-out · E = halftone portrait · F = present day.

## Hook

### Beat 1 (~4s) — 📷 ARCHIVAL PHOTO
**Script:** "On July 30th, 1971, the Union Stock Yards of Chicago"  
**Get it:** Library of Congress (loc.gov/pictures) → search `Union Stock Yards`  
**Fallback image prompt (B&W era photo):** The massive stone entrance gate of a Chicago stockyard with a heavy arched limestone portal, chained shut, empty street in front, scattered paper on the pavement. Camera: wide shot, eye level, centered on the arch. Lighting: flat overcast daylight. Mood: final, somber. Style: 1950s-60s black and white documentary photograph, candid newspaper-photographer look, 35mm lens, soft focus, lifted blacks, heavy film grain, dust specks, one faint vertical scratch, aged silver-gelatin print, faces small or turned away, no text, no logos.  
**Camera angle:** wide shot, eye level, centered on the arch · **Lighting:** flat overcast daylight · **Mood:** final, somber · **Action:** gates standing closed, nobody passing  
**Overlay:** JULY 30, 1971 · CHICAGO  

### Beat 2 (~2s) — 🎬 MODERN STOCK
**Script:** "shut their gates for good."  
**Get it:** Wikimedia Commons, check each licence (commons.wikimedia.org) → search `Union Stock Yard Gate`

Lighting: overcast daylight. Mood: monument to loss. Style: present-day documentary photograph, slightly desaturated muted colours, empty and quiet, natural detail, no people or one distant figure, no text, no logos.  
**Camera angle:** medium wide, eye level · **Lighting:** overcast daylight · **Mood:** monument to loss · **Action:** traffic passes the old gate
